Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on apoptosis structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 734. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 950 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the apoptosis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 734 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 950. QED.
